# Independent review: p-adic measures and Iwasawa algebras

Verdict: **needs_changes**. Reviewer: Codex — codex-J6LwjP. Job `REV-PadicMeasuresIwasawaAlgebras`, issue #146, 2026-10-05. The authenticated claim is comment5986838142, confirmed by bot5986839369. Input repository commit `ffb2a4adef553f144235f619b8ccd2850b1bda17`. The authors of the reviewed input are different workers; this session has not authored or previously reviewed this supplier.

The review independently read all369 inherited node contracts and their source/API/test/prerequisite fields, all350 cited declarations with their actual pinned Lean statements and enclosing telescopes, all quoted source locations, the whole suggested file, all16 source findings, all eight library-audit rows, RS-16 and the listed supplier contracts. Preparation and the designers' reading/compile histories are not treated as independent evidence. No paper is claimed to have been read in full.

The review corrects the clear defects and adds67 mandatory declaration splits. There are436 unchecked nodes: {'construction': 46, 'lemma': 289, 'definition': 12, 'theorem': 55, 'comparison': 34};22 planets;350 baseline declarations;19 gaps;2 supplier requests;8 stages. No stage is marked planned or closed. Checked verdicts: {'verified': 329, 'corrected': 19, 'unverifiable': 88}. Each checked row is in the packet. All88 L4 records retain `unverifiable` because their complete native interface/proof-input/semantic-test contracts remain unestablished, including review-added splits; this does not mean each underlying source theorem is false. Partial/not_read stages are not grounds for rejection.

## Corrections

* Correct the weight-pushforward hypothesis: g is a function on the receiving space Y, while the measure is on X. The suggested `map_weight` already has the right type.
* Correct NSW5.1.10 support to that of the torsion submodule, and add source finding E16. For M=A the displayed original support equality is false; the preceding torsion proof supplies the repair.
* Correct elementary-module uniqueness to the elementary-divisor multisets, not only their total local lengths.
* Define μ on the torsion submodule of an arbitrary finitely generated module. Restrict the tensor-dimension and characteristic-endomorphism formulas to torsion modules. Use ϖ over a ramified coefficient DVR; use O=Z_p where p-normalised examples require it. The change γ↦γ² is a generator change only for odd p.
* Keep the integral character split over a complete DVR containing the character values. Separate the nonsplit Galois-orbit statement, requiring coefficient extensions and integral descent.
* Correct all three resolution ranks together to continuous **homology** H_0=coinvariants,H_1=invariants. The free module gives(1,0,0), the residue field gives(1,2,1). The native exact-resolution interface remains a gap.
* Treat n_0=−1 with ω_−1=1 and degree0. Use the reduced degree of a series in the finite-quotient bound, with preparation giving its monic polynomial representative.
* Add compatible valuation hypotheses to analytic series-zero/identity statements. An arbitrary extension valuation is not sufficient.
* Align the retained six-term sequence, minimal resolution, finiteness criterion and cyclotomic distinguished declaration names with their separate contracts.
* Split dual/reflexive comparisons, pseudo-null and pseudo-isomorphism API, elementary divisors, regular parameters, joint adic completeness, analytic zeros, cyclotomic ideal/limit identities, μ/λ/F, characteristic-divisor/ideal operations, character components, delta controls, growth, free/projective/rank criteria and Euler products. Every added record has `addedBy: REV-PadicMeasuresIwasawaAlgebras`. Exact additions and every changed field are in the embedded diff ledger.
* Correct the LocallyConstant source locator to the actual77-line pinned file. Correct the finite-order norm baseline description to require NormMulClass and NormOneClass as well as NormedRing. No baseline declaration is removed; all350 actual statements are confirmed with their precise scopes.
* In the suggested file, replace the unrestricted ENat-valued function called a characteristic divisor by the native finitely supported natural-valued divisor, with explicit finite-generation/torsion parameters and a local-length specification. Add explicit Module.Finite parameters to μ,λ,F; implicit unused section variables did not bind them. Preserve existing tests and compiler history, clearly label arithmetic controls and current interface limitations.

## Remaining acceptance blockers

1. Normal-domain localisations and duals: common fraction-space embeddings, height-one DVRs, finite torsion support, semilocal PID comparison, localisation of Hom and bidual transport are missing exact inputs.
2. Regular-local dimension/parameter and joint-adic completeness proofs need actual statements. Naming IsRegularLocalRing or a noetherian/UFD instance does not prove them.
3. The native AdjoinRoot power-basis characteristic polynomial equals a **minimal polynomial**. Identifying that polynomial with the defining monic F over a DVR needs a separate proof, including F=1. The indexed `AdjoinRoot.minpoly_root` field theorem cannot be applied to O without adaptation.
4. Cyclotomic quotient inverse and completed-algebra comparison need closed fibres, compactness, quotient identities and Dirac/projection compatibility on the existing owner's carrier. Pure T-adic topology is not an acceptable substitute.
5. Characteristic divisor lengths/support, finite-flat base-change lengths and restriction-of-scalars norm/determinant proofs remain missing. The Bourbaki citation is not independently authenticated; this is recorded rather than silently adopted.
6. Integral split and nonsplit character descent need native projectors and exact module/algebra equivalences. The cited Coates–Sujatha appendix was not independently read. Arithmetic idempotent identities over Q do not establish these integral interfaces.
7. Delta/cyclotomic growth, minimal exact resolutions and resultant/DVR cardinality formulas require native typed carriers, maps and proof inputs. The iterative cyclotomic-primary construction still needs separate termination/classification interfaces.
8. The suggested μ,λ,F signatures only cover Z_p while the packet requires general complete coefficient DVRs. Every exact absent declaration/API name is listed below. Matching anonymous examples by count does not establish semantic tests for these objects.

These are defects of existing L4 contracts and their promised suggested correspondence, separate from the legitimate undecomposed L0a/L5/L6 targets. They prevent acceptance under the issue's rule that every node must be verified, corrected or justified.

## Sources, ownership and tests

All fifteen inherited source issues are independently confirmed, as is new E16. Each has its own verdict and reason in `sourceIssues.review`; the mathematical case ledger records counterexamples and qualifications. The new E16 search is bounded: the author's July28,2026 errata were read fully, and no correction to NSW5.1.10 was found. No priority or exhaustive-search claim is made. Coleman-owned kernel/leading-term slips already registered there were not duplicated.

The source receipt lists URLs, edition SHA256 values and the exact physical pages independently read; page-text hashes enable local comparison without redistributing books. Published RJW pages use printed=physical+99; NSW uses printed=physical−14. Published images24,29,90–91 and NSW273/299 were inspected for the ambiguous notation/indexing. Rowland–Stipulanti–Yassawi is consumed only for q=p>0,0≤r<p. PR41961 is pinned design evidence only, never baseline. The supplier receipt identifies the precise contracts read from ProfiniteProPGroups Layer9, StableReduction Layer1 and ClassicalArithmeticCompletion, plus full RS-16. No foreign roadmap is edited or promoted.

Tests were checked mathematically for signed atoms, zero precision and depth, dyadic sign separation, noncommutative convolution order, refinement sums, bounded Mahler inversion, positive moments versus δ_0, smoothing/regularity and admissible characters. The distinction between algebraic, weak and norm topologies is retained. All definition/construction records have at least three packet tests, including new λ,F,divisor,isotypic,product and delta definitions. Exact typed test correspondence for the L4 objects remains a blocker; scalar polynomial identities alone are insufficient. Planet names remain the22 key objects/theorems, with no source-locator names introduced.

`python3 scripts/check_blueprint.py research/blueprint/packets/PadicMeasuresIwasawaAlgebras.json --index <existing pinned declaration index> --json` reports **0 errors,0 warnings**, including exact indexed baseline names and dependency/reference structure. Additional own inventory checks establish436 unique checked node IDs,67 unique reviewer-added IDs,16 source verdicts and350 confirmed baseline receipts. These checks do not prove mathematics.

**Fresh full-file Lean elaboration was not run.** No authenticated existing build containing both pinned Tau Ceti imports was located. No Lake setup/cache/build, language server or Lean code outside the allowed suggested file was used. The inherited successful full compile and740 admission warnings remain historical evidence only; they do not validate this modified file or its missing interfaces.

For the orchestrator: route the named L4 inputs and signature/test completion as fixes before acceptance; authenticate the Bourbaki and Coates–Sujatha inputs or replace their source routes. Resolve the declared L4-consumer/L5-topological-Nakayama architecture issue without creating a dependency cycle. Keep honest partial stages as follow-up work. This review is submitted complete with a needs_changes verdict; nothing is promoted.

After submission the private primary copies and job scratch are deleted. All durable handoff evidence is embedded below, so no private paths or deleted scratch files are required. To inspect an artifact, base64-decode its block then zlib-decompress it; the SHA256 is for the exact uncompressed UTF-8 bytes. The packets' review rows and source verdicts are also directly readable JSON.
